<div class="eli-container">

<div id="tit_1" class="eli-main-title">

COMMISSION IMPLEMENTING REGULATION (EU) 2026/1731

of 15 July 2026

amending Implementing Regulations (EU) 2024/2977, (EU) 2024/2979, (EU) 2024/2980 and (EU) 2024/2982 as regards applicable standards and specifications

</div>

<div id="pbl_1" class="eli-subdivision">

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION,

<div id="cit_1" class="eli-subdivision">

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union,

</div>

<div id="cit_2" class="eli-subdivision">

Having regard to Regulation (EU) No 910/2014 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 23 July 2014 on electronic identification and trust services for electronic transactions in the internal market and repealing Directive 1999/93/EC <a href="#ntr1-L_202601731EN.000101-E0001" id="ntc1-L_202601731EN.000101-E0001">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>, and in particular Article 5a(23) thereof,

</div>

Whereas:

<div id="rct_1" class="eli-subdivision">

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(1)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>To ensure the highest level of harmonisation among Member States for the development of European Digital Identity Wallets, the technical specifications for the wallets rely on the work carried out on the basis of Commission Recommendation (EU) 2021/946 <a href="#ntr2-L_202601731EN.000101-E0002" id="ntc2-L_202601731EN.000101-E0002">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">2</span>)</a> and in particular the architecture and reference framework. As the architecture and reference framework has evolved significantly since Commission Implementing Regulations (EU) 2024/2977 <a href="#ntr3-L_202601731EN.000101-E0003" id="ntc3-L_202601731EN.000101-E0003">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">3</span>)</a>, (EU) 2024/2979 <a href="#ntr4-L_202601731EN.000101-E0004" id="ntc4-L_202601731EN.000101-E0004">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">4</span>)</a>, (EU) 2024/2980 <a href="#ntr5-L_202601731EN.000101-E0005" id="ntc5-L_202601731EN.000101-E0005">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">5</span>)</a>, and (EU) 2024/2982 <a href="#ntr6-L_202601731EN.000101-E0006" id="ntc6-L_202601731EN.000101-E0006">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">6</span>)</a>, those Implementing Regulations should now be amended to align them with new standards, specifications and procedures.</p>
<p>In accordance with the objectives of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014, a number of standards have been selected to meet these specific requirements. These standards should reflect established practices and be widely recognised within the relevant sectors. For example, as the W3C VCDM format is used as the reference format for attestations in particular in the educational sector, the European Digital Identity Wallets should also support this format when the new profiles on the W3C VCDM format are available. Where necessary, these standards should be adapted or complemented in order to ensure the security and trustworthiness of European Digital Identity Wallets, while facilitating cross-border interoperability and the effective functioning of the internal market.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

<div id="rct_2" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(2\) | For any use of the wallet that requires the presentation of the wallet user’s portrait, the wallet solutions must support the functionality of selective disclosure and disclosure must be under the full control of the user. To protect the ability to decide upon disclosure, and the portrait against unintended or unauthorised request for disclosure the architectural design of the European Digital Identity Wallets should provide for warning mechanisms and logging all transactions related to the use of the portrait. To ensure that the wallet user is aware of sharing biometric data, the warnings should indicate that the request involves the sharing of biometric data and specifically require the user to confirm disclosure. Where a relying party processes the portrait for the purpose of uniquely identifying a natural person or for confirming that person’s claimed identity, Article 6 and 9 of Regulation (EU) 2016/679 of the European Parliament and of the Council <a href="#ntr7-L_202601731EN.000101-E0007" id="ntc7-L_202601731EN.000101-E0007">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">7</span>)</a> apply as well as all other requirements of that Regulation, including that the processing of the portrait by relying parties should be limited to what is necessary for intended use. The intended use should be communicated to the wallet user, together with the request for disclosure, in clear and intelligible language. To duly consider the sensitivity of biometric data, the wallet user should explicitly and specifically confirm the disclosure of the portrait. Silence or pre-ticked boxes should not be considered as confirmation by the wallet user. The explicit confirmation of the wallet user should be a technical safeguard and not in itself provide a legal ground for processing. As set out in paragraph 4 of Article 9 of Regulation (EU) 2016/679 Member States may maintain or introduce further conditions, including limitations, with regard to the processing of genetic data, biometric data or data concerning health. |

</div>

<div id="rct_3" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(3\) | To give Member States sufficient time to adapt their national procedures, the wallet user’s portrait may be part of the mandatory person identification data for the natural person only as of 11 August 2028. Where these images are sourced from existing identity documents, such as identity cards or passports, the relevant requirements laid down in Council Regulations (EU) 2025/1208 <a href="#ntr8-L_202601731EN.000101-E0008" id="ntc8-L_202601731EN.000101-E0008">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">8</span>)</a> or (EC) No 2252/2004 <a href="#ntr9-L_202601731EN.000101-E0009" id="ntc9-L_202601731EN.000101-E0009">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">9</span>)</a> respectively apply. |

</div>

<div id="rct_4" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(4\) | Regulation (EU) No 910/2014 requires that wallets are capable of displaying an EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark, as a verifiable, simple, and recognisable indication that a wallet has been provided in accordance with the Regulation. The use of such a Trust Mark will support the effective functioning of the internal market, guarantee fair competition and protect consumer interests. To enable the use of such a Trust Mark, the visual and technical characteristics of the Trust Mark should be established. |

</div>

<div id="rct_5" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(5\) | As set out in Article 12b of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014, gatekeepers are to allow providers of European Digital Identity Wallets and issuers of notified electronic identification means effective interoperability with, and, for the purposes of interoperability, access to, the same operating system, hardware or software features. Such effective interoperability and access are to be provided free of charge and irrespective of whether those hardware or software features form part of the operating system, are available to, or are used by, that gatekeeper when providing such services. As all wallet solutions should support a common set of protocols and interfaces in order to ensure usability, security and interoperability across Member States, gatekeepers should enable the operating system, hardware or software features necessary to implement the protocols and interfaces set out in Annex XII to this Regulation. In this context, in online cross-device flows, for both the physical proximity check and data transfer between the two devices, a local communication channel as enabled by the Client To Authenticator Protocol (CTAP) specification version 2.3 <a href="#ntr10-L_202601731EN.000101-E0010" id="ntc10-L_202601731EN.000101-E0010">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">10</span>)</a> should be preferred by gatekeepers to using CTAP Hybrid tunnel services. |

</div>

<div id="rct_6" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(6\) | To give Member States, providers of wallet-relying party registration certificates and wallet providers sufficient time to enable wallet units to authenticate and validate wallet-relying party registration certificates, this requirement should only apply as of 11 August 2028. |

</div>

<div id="rct_7" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(7\) | Regulation (EU) 2016/679 and, where relevant, Directive 2002/58/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council <a href="#ntr11-L_202601731EN.000101-E0011" id="ntc11-L_202601731EN.000101-E0011">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">11</span>)</a> apply to all personal data processing activities under this Regulation. |

</div>

<div id="rct_8" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(8\) | The European Data Protection Supervisor was consulted in accordance with Article 42(1) of Regulation (EU) 2018/1725 of the European Parliament and of the Council <a href="#ntr12-L_202601731EN.000101-E0012" id="ntc12-L_202601731EN.000101-E0012">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">12</span>)</a> and delivered its opinion on 17 April 2026 <a href="#ntr13-L_202601731EN.000101-E0013" id="ntc13-L_202601731EN.000101-E0013">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">13</span>)</a>. |

</div>

<div id="rct_9" class="eli-subdivision">

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(9\) | The measures provided for in this Regulation are in accordance with the opinion of the committee established by Article 48 of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014, |

</div>

HAS ADOPTED THIS REGULATION:

</div>

<div id="enc_1" class="eli-subdivision">

<div id="art_1" class="eli-subdivision">

Article 1

<div id="art_1.tit_1" class="eli-title">

Amendments to Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2977

</div>

Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2977 is amended as follows:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(1)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The following Article 3a is inserted:</p>
<div id="003A">
<p>‘Article 3a</p>
<p>Protection of the portrait</p>
<div id="003A.001">
<p>1.   In addition to information requirements pursuant to Regulation (EU) 2016/679, wallet providers shall ensure that the wallet solutions they provide issue warnings to wallet users where wallet-relying parties request the disclosure of the portrait, indicating that the request involves the sharing of biometric data and requires confirmation for the selective disclosure of the portrait.</p>
</div>
<div id="003A.002">
<p>2.   For the implementation of selectively disclosing the portrait to a wallet-relying party, the wallet providers shall ensure the wallet solutions require the wallet user to explicitly and specifically confirm the presentation of the portrait.</p>
</div>
<div id="003A.003">
<p>3.   The portrait shall not be retained by wallet-relying parties unless its processing is necessary for the purposes of identification and authentication in compliance with Union data protection law or where this is provided for by Union or national law, in compliance with Union data protection law. The portrait shall not be transferred to third countries or international organisations unless permitted by Union data protection law.’</p>
</div>
</div>
;</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(2)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>in Article 4, paragraph 1 is replaced by the following:</p>
<div id="004.001">
<p>‘1.   Electronic attestations of attributes issued to wallet units shall comply with at least one of the standards set out in Annex II of Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2979.’</p>
</div>
;</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(3)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>in Article 5, paragraph 4, point (b) is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘(b)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>where the wallet unit attestation of the wallet unit to which the person identification data was issued to has been revoked;’;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

|  |  |
|----|----|
| \(4\) | The Annex is replaced by the text set out in Annex I to this Regulation. |

</div>

| \(8\) | in Article 14, paragraph 1 is deleted; |

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(9)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the following Article 14a is inserted:</p>
<div id="014A">
<p>‘Article 14a</p>
<p>EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark</p>
<div id="014A.001">
<p>1.   Wallet providers shall ensure that wallet units display the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark. The EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark shall be in the form set out in Annexes VI and VII.</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.002">
<p>2.   Wallet providers shall ensure that wallet units enable wallet users to access information allowing them to verify the certification status of the wallet solution. For that purpose, wallet providers shall ensure that, following the registration of a wallet solution, the corresponding wallet units include the URLs provided by the European Commission for such verification. Wallet providers shall ensure that their wallet units have access to EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark data that comply with the technical specifications set out in Annex VIII.</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.003">
<p>3.   The reference colours for the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark shall be Pantone No 661 and 116, or blue (100 % cyan + 67 % magenta + 0 % yellow + 40 % black) and yellow (0 % cyan + 20 % magenta + 100 % yellow + 0 % black), when a four colour process is used; when RGB colours are used the reference colours shall be blue (0 red + 51 green + 153 blue) and yellow (255 red + 204 green + 0 blue).</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.004">
<p>4.   Only where the use of colour is not practicable, the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark may be used in black and white as set out in Annex VII.</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.005">
<p>5.   Where the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark is used on a dark background, it may be used in negative format using the same background colour. Where the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark is used in colour on a coloured background that makes it difficult to see it, a delimiting outer line around the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark may be used to improve contrast with the background colours.</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.006">
<p>6.   The EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark shall have a minimum size of 64 × 85 pixels at 150 dpi.</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.007">
<p>7.   Wallet providers shall ensure that the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark is used in a manner enabling the clear indication of the wallet unit that the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark pertains to. The EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark may be associated with graphic or textual elements clearly indicating the wallet unit it is used for, provided that they do not change its recognisability as an EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark, nor alter the association with the list of certified European Digital Identity Wallets referred to in Article 5d of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014.</p>
</div>
<div id="014A.008">
<p>8.   Where wallet providers have revoked a wallet unit attestation, they shall ensure that the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark is no longer displayed by the corresponding wallet unit.’</p>
</div>
</div>
.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<div id="art_3" class="eli-subdivision">

Article 3

<div id="art_3.tit_1" class="eli-title">

Amendments to Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980

</div>

Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980 is amended as follows:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">

<col style="width: 4%" />

<td data-valign="top"><p>1.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>in Article 5, paragraph 2 is replaced by the following:</p>
<div id="005.002">
<p>‘2.   Where applicable, the Commission shall establish, maintain and publish a list compiling the information notified by Member States on wallet providers, providers of person identification data, providers of wallet-relying party access certificates and providers of wallet-relying party registration certificates, as referred to in Annex II, sections 2, 3, 4 and 5.’</p>
</div>
;</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

|  |  |
|----|----|
| 2\. | Annex II to Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980 is amended as set out in Annex X to this Regulation. |

</div>

;</td>

<div id="004.001">
<p>‘1.   Wallet providers shall ensure that wallet solutions support protocols and interfaces for the presentation of attributes to wallet-relying parties, remotely, and, where appropriate, in proximity, in accordance with the technical specifications set out in Annex II.</p>
</div>
<div id="004.002">
<p>2.   Wallet providers shall ensure that, at the request of users, wallet units respond to successfully authenticated and validated requests from wallet-relying parties referred to in Article 3, in accordance with the technical specifications set out in Annex II.’</p>
</div>
;</td>

<div id="008">
<p>‘Article 8</p>
<p>Entry into force</p>
<p>This Regulation shall enter into force on the twentieth day following that of its publication in the Official Journal of the European Union.</p>
<p>Article 3(4) shall apply from 11 August 2028.</p>
<p>This Regulation shall be binding in its entirety and directly applicable in all Member States.’</p>
</div>
.</td>

<div id="art_5" class="eli-subdivision">

Article 5

<div id="art_5.tit_1" class="eli-title">

Entry into force

</div>

This Regulation shall enter into force on the twentieth day following that of its publication in the Official Journal of the European Union.

This Regulation shall be binding in its entirety and directly applicable in all Member States.

</div>

</div>

Done at Brussels, 15 July 2026.

<span class="oj-italic">The President</span>

Ursula VON DER LEYEN

</div>

<div id="L_202601731EN.000801.fmx">

<div id="anx_I" class="eli-container">

ANNEX I

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

ANNEX

Technical specifications for person identification data referred to in Article 3(3)

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

1.   

Section 1: Set of natural person identification data

<span class="oj-italic">Table 1</span>

<span class="oj-bold">Mandatory person identification data for the natural person for selective disclosure</span>

|  |  |
|----|----|
| Data identifier | Definition |
| family_name | Current last name(s) or surname(s) of the user to whom the person identification data relates. |
| given_name | Current first name(s), including middle name(s) where applicable, of the user to whom the person identification data relates. |
| birth_date | Day, month, and year on which the user to whom the person identification data relates was born. |
| birth_place | The country as an alpha-2 country code as specified in ISO 3166-1, or the state, province, district, or local area or the municipality, city, town, or village where the user to whom the person identification data relates was born. |
| nationality | One or more alpha-2 country codes as specified in ISO 3166-1, representing the nationality of the user to whom the person identification data relates. |
| portrait | Except where the user explicitly opts out, where applicable, the facial image of the user to whom the person identification data relates, compliant with the quality requirements for a full frontal image type as set out in ISO/IEC 39794-5 or, for backward compatibility, ISO/IEC 19794-5, clauses 8.2, 8.3 and 8.4, provided as encoded image data without the headers or blocks as specified in clause 5 of ISO/IEC 19794-5, except for the image data itself (a JPEG) shall apply from 11 August 2028. |

|  |  |
|----|----|
| — | Member States may provide that the user has the option to decline the insertion of the portrait to the person identification data. |

|  |  |
|----|----|
| — | Member States shall ensure that the selective disclosure applies to each data identifier, including the portrait. |

|  |  |
|----|----|
| — | Where the birth date of the natural person is not known, Member States shall choose appropriate values that comply with the specifications set out in sections 4.1 or 4.2 (as appropriate) to this Annex. |

|  |  |
|----|----|
| — | Where the nationality of the natural person is unknown, Member States shall use the value 'QU'. |

|  |  |
|----|----|
| — | Where the natural person does not hold a nationality, Member States shall use the value 'QS'. |

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Where the user opts out of the inclusion of the portrait, Member States shall set the value empty.</p>
<p><span class="oj-italic">Table 2</span></p>
<p><span class="oj-bold">Optional person identification data for the natural person for selective disclosure</span></p>
<table class="oj-table" width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 68%" />
<col style="width: 32%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Data identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Definition</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>The full address of the place where the user to whom the person identification data relates currently resides or can be contacted (street name, house number, city, etc.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>The country where the user to whom the person identification data relates currently resides, as an alpha-2 country code as specified in ISO 3166-1.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_state</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>The state, province, district, or local area where the user to whom the person identification data relates currently resides.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_city</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>The municipality, city, town, or village where the user to whom the person identification data relates currently resides.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_postal_code</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>The postal code of the place where the user to whom the person identification data relates currently resides.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_street</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>The name of the street where the user to whom the person identification data relates currently resides, including the house number and any affix or suffix thereof.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>personal_administrative_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>A value assigned to the user to whom the person identification data relates that is unique among all personal administrative numbers issued by the provider of person identification data. Where Member States opt to include this attribute, they shall describe in their electronic identification schemes under which the person identification data is issued, the policy that they apply to the values of this attribute, including, where applicable, specific conditions for the processing of this value.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Last name(s) or surname(s) of the user to whom the person identification data relates at the time of birth.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>First name(s), including middle name(s), of the user to whom the person identification data relates at the time of birth.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>sex</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Values shall be one of the following:</p>
<p>0 = not known;</p>
<p>1 = male;</p>
<p>2 = female;</p>
<p>3 = other;</p>
<p>4 = inter;</p>
<p>5 = diverse;</p>
<p>6 = open;</p>
<p>9 = not applicable;</p>
<p>For values 0, 1, 2 and 9, ISO/IEC 5218 applies.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>email_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Electronic mail address of the user to whom the person identification data relates [in accordance with RFC 5322<a href="#ntr1-L_202601731EN.000802-E0001" id="ntc1-L_202601731EN.000802-E0001"> (<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>].</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>mobile_phone_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Mobile telephone number of the user to whom the person identification data relates, starting with the ‘+’ symbol as the international code prefix and the country code, followed by numbers only.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-tblnote">
<td colspan="2"><p><a href="#ntc1-L_202601731EN.000802-E0001" id="ntr1-L_202601731EN.000802-E0001">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>  P. Resnick, Ed., ‘Internet Message Format,’ RFC 5322, October 2008.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

2.   

Section 2: Set of legal person identification data

<span class="oj-italic">Table 3</span>

<span class="oj-bold">Mandatory person identification data for the legal person</span>

|  |
|----|
| Data identifier |
| current legal name |
| a unique identifier constructed by the sending Member State in accordance with the technical specifications for the purposes of cross-border identification and which is as persistent as possible in time |

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Where a data identifier is not known for the person or cannot otherwise be issued as part of the person identification dataset, Member States shall instead use an attribute value appropriate to the situation.</p>
<p><span class="oj-italic">Table 4</span></p>
<p><span class="oj-bold">Optional person identification data for the legal person</span></p>
<table class="oj-table" width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 100%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Data identifier</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>current address</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>VAT registration number</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tax reference number</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>European unique identifier referred to in Directive (EU) 2017/1132 of the European Parliament and of the Council<a href="#ntr2-L_202601731EN.000802-E0002" id="ntc2-L_202601731EN.000802-E0002"> (<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">2</span>)</a></p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Legal Entity Identifier (LEI) referred to in Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2022/1860<a href="#ntr3-L_202601731EN.000802-E0003" id="ntc3-L_202601731EN.000802-E0003"> (<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">3</span>)</a></p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Economic Operator Registration and Identification (EORI) referred to in Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 1352/2013<a href="#ntr4-L_202601731EN.000802-E0004" id="ntc4-L_202601731EN.000802-E0004"> (<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">4</span>)</a></p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>excise number provided in Article 2(12) of Council Regulation (EU) No 389/2012<a href="#ntr5-L_202601731EN.000802-E0005" id="ntc5-L_202601731EN.000802-E0005"> (<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">5</span>)</a></p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-tblnote">
<td><p><a href="#ntc2-L_202601731EN.000802-E0002" id="ntr2-L_202601731EN.000802-E0002">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">2</span>)</a>  Directive (EU) 2017/1132 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 14 June 2017 relating to certain aspects of company law (<a href="http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2017_169_R_TOC">OJ L 169, 30.6.2017, p. 46</a>, ELI: <a href="http://data.europa.eu/eli/dir/2017/1132/oj">http://data.europa.eu/eli/dir/2017/1132/oj</a>).</p>
<p><a href="#ntc3-L_202601731EN.000802-E0003" id="ntr3-L_202601731EN.000802-E0003">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">3</span>)</a>  Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2022/1860 of 10 June 2022 laying down implementing technical standards for the application of Regulation (EU) No 648/2012 of the European Parliament and of the Council with regard to the standards, formats, frequency and methods and arrangements for reporting (<a href="http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2022_262_R_TOC">OJ L 262, 7.10.2022, p. 68</a>, ELI: <a href="http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2022/1860/oj">http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2022/1860/oj</a>).</p>
<p><a href="#ntc4-L_202601731EN.000802-E0004" id="ntr4-L_202601731EN.000802-E0004">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">4</span>)</a>  Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 1352/2013 of 4 December 2013 establishing the forms provided for in Regulation (EU) No 608/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council concerning customs enforcement of intellectual property rights (<a href="http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2013_341_R_TOC">OJ L 341, 18.12.2013, p. 10</a>, ELI: <a href="http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2013/1352/oj">http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2013/1352/oj</a>).</p>
<p><a href="#ntc5-L_202601731EN.000802-E0005" id="ntr5-L_202601731EN.000802-E0005">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">5</span>)</a>  C ouncil Regulation (EU) No 389/2012 of 2 May 2012 on administrative cooperation in the field of excise duties and repealing Regulation (EC) No 2073/2004 (<a href="http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2012_121_R_TOC">OJ L 121, 8.5.2012, p. 1</a>, ELI: <a href="http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg/2012/389/oj">http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg/2012/389/oj</a>).</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

3.   

Section 3: Set of metadata about person identification data

<span class="oj-italic">Table 5</span>

<span class="oj-bold">Metadata about the person identification data</span>

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
| Data identifier | Definition | Presence |
| issuing_authority | Name of the administrative authority that issued the person identification data, or the ISO 3166 alpha-2 country code of the respective Member State if there is no separate authority entitled to issue person identification data. | mandatory |
| issuing_country | alpha-2 country code, as specified in ISO 3166-1, of the country or territory of the provider of the person identification data. | mandatory |
| expiry_date | Date (and if possible time) when the administrative validity period of the person identification data will expire. | optional |
| document_number | A number for the person identification data, assigned by the provider of person identification data. | optional |
| issuing_jurisdiction | Country subdivision code of the jurisdiction that issued the person identification data, as specified in ISO 3166-2:2020, Clause 8. The first part of the code shall be the same as the value for the issuing country. | optional |
| issuance_date | Date, and if possible time, when the administrative validity period of the person identification data started. | optional |

</div>

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

4.   

Section 4: Encoding of natural person identification data attributes

|  |  |
|----|----|
| — | Natural person identification data shall be issued in accordance with the standards set out in Annex II to Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2979, clauses 5 (SD-JWT VC format) and 6 (ISO/IEC-mdoc format) as applicable to electronic attestations of attributes. Clauses 5.2.2, 5.2.4, 5.2.5, EAA-6.1-03, 6.2.2, 6.2.3,6.2.4 and 6.2.5 shall not apply. |

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The encoding of natural person identification data shall comply with the technical specifications in sections 4.1 and 4.2 of this Annex.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Encoding of natural person identification data in ISO/IEC-mdoc format</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The attestation type for person identification data in ISO/IEC mdoc format shall be 'eu.europa.ec.eudi.pid.1'. The identifier of the namespace for the person identification data attributes set out in this Annex shall be 'eu.europa.ec.eudi.pid.1'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Where the person identification data includes data for which data identifiers are not set out in this Annex, these data shall be defined within a domestic person identification data namespace that uses the general format eu.europa.ec.eudi.pid.[ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code or the ISO 3166-2 region code] followed by an optional dot and version number.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Where the domestic namespace is used, its scheme, including all data identifiers, their definitions, presence and encoding formats, shall be published in accordance with Article 8 of Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2025/1569 <a href="#ntr6-L_202601731EN.000802-E0006" id="ntc6-L_202601731EN.000802-E0006">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">6</span>)</a>.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The person identification data and its metadata set out in sections 1 and 3 of this Annex shall be included in person identification data elements within the meaning of ISO/IEC mdoc format specifications.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The deviceKey member within the deviceKeyInfo member of the instance of MobileSecurityObject type shall contain a public key.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>That public key shall correspond to a private key that is stored in the wallet secure cryptographic device (‘WSCD’) of the wallet user.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The Protected Header of the CB-AdES digital signature signing a person identification data in ISO/IEC-mdoc format shall contain the x5u and the x5t header parameters, both specified in RFC 9360 <a href="#ntr7-L_202601731EN.000802-E0007" id="ntc7-L_202601731EN.000802-E0007">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">7</span>)</a>.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The digest algorithm used in the x5t header parameter shall be SHA-256.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The requirements for encoding person identification data in ISO/IEC-mdoc format are set out in Table 6:</p>
<p><span class="oj-italic">Table 6</span></p>
<p><span class="oj-bold">Requirements for encoding person identification data in ISO/IEC-mdoc format</span></p>
<table class="oj-table" width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 41%" />
<col style="width: 41%" />
<col style="width: 19%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Data Identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Attribute identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Encoding format</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>full-date</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_place</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>place_of_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>place_of_birth</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>nationality</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>nationality</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>nationalities</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_state</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_state</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_city</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_city</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_postal_code</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_postal_code</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_street</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_street</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>personal_administrative_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>personal_administrative_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>portrait</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>portrait</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>bstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>sex</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>sex</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>uint</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>email_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>email_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>mobile_phone_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>mobile_phone_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>expiry_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>expiry_date</p></td>

<p>or full-date</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_authority</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_authority</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>document_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>document_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_jurisdiction</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_jurisdiction</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tstr</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuance_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuance_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>tdate</p>
<p>or full-date</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The notation of the encoding format of the attributes specified in Table 6 shall use representation types as specified in RFC 8610 <a href="#ntr8-L_202601731EN.000802-E0008" id="ntc8-L_202601731EN.000802-E0008">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">8</span>)</a>, with the following additional requirements:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(a)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a tstr shall be encoded in UTF-8;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(b)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a tstr shall support the full Unicode range;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(c)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a tstr shall have a maximum length of 150 characters;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(d)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a date shall be encoded as specified in RFC 8943 <a href="#ntr9-L_202601731EN.000802-E0009" id="ntc9-L_202601731EN.000802-E0009">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">9</span>)</a>;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(e)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a full-date shall be interpreted as #6.1004(tstr), where tag 1004 is as specified in RFC 8943;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(f)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a tdate attribute shall contain a date-time string as specified in RFC 3339 <a href="#ntr10-L_202601731EN.000802-E0010" id="ntc10-L_202601731EN.000802-E0010">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">10</span>)</a>;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(g)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a full-date attribute shall contain a full-date string as specified in RFC 3339, in accordance with RFC 8943;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(h)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a representation of a date in attributes shall, unless otherwise indicated:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>not use fractions of seconds;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>not use a local offset from UTC and the time-offset specified in RFC 3339 shall be set to 'Z'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(i)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>an integer with major types 0 and 1 shall be as small as possible, as specified in RFC 8949 <a href="#ntr11-L_202601731EN.000802-E0011" id="ntc11-L_202601731EN.000802-E0011">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">11</span>)</a>, section 4.2;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(j)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a place_of_birth shall contain at least one of the following key-value pairs: 'country', 'region', or 'locality';</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(k)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the expression of the length in a bstr, tstr, array or map shall be as short as possible, as specified in RFC 8949, section 4.2;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(l)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the attribute nationality shall be encoded as an array of alpha-2 country codes as specified in ISO 3166-1. Where CDDL notation as specified in RFC 8610 is used, the encoding of this attribute shall be:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>nationalities = [+ CountryCode];</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>CountryCode = tstr; alpha-2 country code specified in ISO 3166-1;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>where a wallet user to whom the person identification data relates has multiple nationalities and the provider of person identification data attests to these multiple nationalities, the provider of person identification data may include all the nationalities in the person identification data;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the attribute place_of_birth shall be encoded as a type place_of_birth. Where CDDL notation as specified in RFC 8610 is used, the encoding of this attribute shall be:</p>
<p>place_of_birth =</p>
<p>{</p>
<p>? 'country': tstr; a single alpha-2 country code as specified in ISO 3166-1</p>
<p>? 'region': tstr; the name of a state, province, district, or local area</p>
<p>? 'locality': tstr; the name of a municipality, city, town, or village</p>
<p>}</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements for encoding person identification data in SD-JWT VC format</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The person identification data and its metadata specified in this section shall be included in a person identification data as claims in the meaning of SD-JWT VC format specifications.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>All claims in the issued person identification data, referenced in the previous indent, shall be selectively disclosable individually, except those claims defined as non-selectively disclosable in SD-JWT VC format.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Table 7 specifies the encoding of claim names that are public names.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Table 8 specifies the encoding of claim names that are specific to the person identification data.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>A JSON string used in person identification data encoded in SD-JWT VC shall be encoded in UTF-8 and shall support the full Unicode range, unless explicitly specified otherwise in Table 8 below or the references therein.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The JWT claims nbf and exp as defined in RFC 7519 <a href="#ntr12-L_202601731EN.000802-E0012" id="ntc12-L_202601731EN.000802-E0012">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">12</span>)</a> shall be used to express the technical validity period of person identification data compliant with SD-JWT VC format.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Person identification data shall incorporate the cnf claim as defined in RFC 7800 <a href="#ntr13-L_202601731EN.000802-E0013" id="ntc13-L_202601731EN.000802-E0013">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">13</span>)</a>, which shall be a public key generated from a private key stored in the WSCD of the wallet users wallet unit.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The Protected Header of the digital signature signing a person identification data in SD-JWT VC format shall contain the x5u and the x5t#S256 header parameters, specified in RFC 7515 <a href="#ntr14-L_202601731EN.000802-E0014" id="ntc14-L_202601731EN.000802-E0014">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">14</span>)</a>.</p>
<p><span class="oj-italic">Table 7</span></p>
<p><span class="oj-bold">Requirements for encoding person identification data in SD-JWT VC format using public names</span></p>
<table class="oj-table" width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 36%" />
<col style="width: 39%" />
<col style="width: 25%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Data Identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Attribute identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Encoding format</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birthdate</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string, ISO 8601-1, YYYY-MM-DD format</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_place</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>place_of_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>JSON structure</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>nationality</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>nationalities</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>array of strings</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>address.formatted</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>address.country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_state</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>address.region</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_city</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>address.locality</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_postal_code</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>address.postal_code</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>resident_street</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>address.street_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>family_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_family_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>given_name_birth</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>birth_given_name</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>email_address</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>email</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>mobile_phone_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>phone_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>portrait</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>picture</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string; data URL containing a base64-encoded portrait in JPEG format</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p><span class="oj-italic">Table 8</span></p>
<p><span class="oj-bold">Requirements for encoding person identification data in SD-JWT VC format using private names</span></p>
<table class="oj-table" width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 43%" />
<col style="width: 43%" />
<col style="width: 14%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Data Identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Attribute identifier</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>Encoding format</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>expiry_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>date_of_expiry</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string, ISO 8601-1, YYYY-MM-DD format</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuance_date</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>date_of_issuance</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string, ISO 8601-1, YYYY-MM-DD format</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>personal_administrative_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>personal_administrative_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>sex</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>sex</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>number</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_authority</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_authority</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_country</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>document_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>document_number</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="oj-table">
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_jurisdiction</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>issuing_jurisdiction</p></td>
<td class="oj-table" data-valign="top"><p>string</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The base type of person identification data shall be 'urn:eudi:pid:1', included in the vct claim. All person identification data shall use types in the namespace 'urn:eudi:pid:'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Where person identification data includes attributes that are not specified in this Annex, these attributes shall be defined within a domestic type.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Where the domestic type is used, its scheme, including all data identifiers, their definitions, presence and encoding formats, shall be defined in a scheme that is published in accordance with Article 8 of Implementing Regulation (EU) 2025/1569.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

5.   

Section 5: Trust infrastructure details

The list of providers of person identification data made available by the Commission in accordance with Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980 shall enable the authentication of person identification data.

</div>

</div>

</div>

’

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<a href="#ntc6-L_202601731EN.000802-E0006" id="ntr6-L_202601731EN.000802-E0006">(<span class="oj-super">6</span>)</a>  Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2025/1569 of 29 July 2025 laying down rules for the application of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014 of the European Parliament and of the Council as regards qualified electronic attestations of attributes and electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source ([OJ L, 2025/1569, 30.7.2025, ELI: http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2025/1569/oj](https://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2025/1569/oj)).

<a href="#ntc7-L_202601731EN.000802-E0007" id="ntr7-L_202601731EN.000802-E0007">(<span class="oj-super">7</span>)</a>  J. Schaad, ‘CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Header Parameters for Carrying and Referencing X.509 Certificates’ (<https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/rfc9360/>).

<a href="#ntc8-L_202601731EN.000802-E0008" id="ntr8-L_202601731EN.000802-E0008">(<span class="oj-super">8</span>)</a>  C. Vigano and H. Birkholz, ‘Concise Data Definition Language (CDDL): A Notational Convention to Express Concise Binary Object Representation (CBOR) and JSON Data Structures’, RFC 8610, June 2019.

<a href="#ntc9-L_202601731EN.000802-E0009" id="ntr9-L_202601731EN.000802-E0009">(<span class="oj-super">9</span>)</a>  M. Jones, A. Nadalin and J. Richter, ‘Concise Binary Object Representation (CBOR) Tags for Date’, RFC 8943, November 2020.

<a href="#ntc10-L_202601731EN.000802-E0010" id="ntr10-L_202601731EN.000802-E0010">(<span class="oj-super">10</span>)</a>  G. Klyne and C. Newman, ‘Date and Time on the Internet: Timestamps’, RFC 3339, July 2002.

<a href="#ntc11-L_202601731EN.000802-E0011" id="ntr11-L_202601731EN.000802-E0011">(<span class="oj-super">11</span>)</a>  C. Bormann and P. Hoffman, ‘Concise Binary Object Representation (CBOR)’, RFC 8949, December 2020.

<a href="#ntc12-L_202601731EN.000802-E0012" id="ntr12-L_202601731EN.000802-E0012">(<span class="oj-super">12</span>)</a>  J. Jones, et al., ‘JSON Web Token (JWT)’, RFC 7519, May 2015.

<a href="#ntc13-L_202601731EN.000802-E0013" id="ntr13-L_202601731EN.000802-E0013">(<span class="oj-super">13</span>)</a>  M. Jones, et al., ‘Proof-of-Possession Key Semantics for JSON Web Tokens (JWTs)’, RFC 7800, April 2016.

<a href="#ntc14-L_202601731EN.000802-E0014" id="ntr14-L_202601731EN.000802-E0014">(<span class="oj-super">14</span>)</a>  M. Jones, et al., ‘JSON Web Signature (JWS)’, RFC 7515, May 2015.

</div>

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<div id="L_202601731EN.001701.fmx">

<div id="anx_II" class="eli-container">

ANNEX II

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

ANNEX Ia

Cryptographic mechanisms referred to in Article 5a

European Cybersecurity Certification Group, Sub-group on Cryptography: “Agreed Cryptographic Mechanisms” published by the European Union Agency for Cybersecurity (“ENISA”) <a href="#ntr1-L_202601731EN.001702-E0001" id="ntc1-L_202601731EN.001702-E0001">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>.

</div>

</div>

’

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<a href="#ntc1-L_202601731EN.001702-E0001" id="ntr1-L_202601731EN.001702-E0001">(<span class="oj-super">1</span>)</a>   <https://certification.enisa.europa.eu/publications/eucc-guidelines-cryptography_en>.

</div>

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<div id="L_202601731EN.001801.fmx">

<div id="anx_III" class="eli-container">

ANNEX III

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

ANNEX Ib

Technical specifications for wallet unit attestations referred to in Article 6(2a)

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

1.   

A wallet unit attestation shall comprise one or more wallet instance attestations and one or more key attestations.

</div>

<div class="oj-enumeration-spacing">

2.   

The wallet instance attestation and the key attestations shall meet the following requirements:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(a)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Format requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>FR-WIA-1: A wallet instance attestation shall be a JSON Web Token (JWT) as specified in RFC 7519 <a href="#ntr1-L_202601731EN.001802-E0001" id="ntc1-L_202601731EN.001802-E0001">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>, signed or sealed by the wallet provider by means of compact JAdES baseline B signature.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>FR-WIA-1.1: A wallet instance attestation shall be a wallet attestation as specified in Appendix E of OpenID for Verifiable Credential Issuance v1.0 <a href="#ntr2-L_202601731EN.001802-E0002" id="ntc2-L_202601731EN.001802-E0002">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">2</span>)</a> ('OID4VCI') and extended as specified in C-WIA-1 and C-WIA-2 below.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>FR-KA-1: A key attestation shall be a JWT as specified in RFC 7519, signed or sealed by the wallet provider by means of compact JAdES baseline B signature.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>FR_KA_1.1: A key attestation shall be a key attestation as specified in Appendix D of OID4VCI, extended as specified in C_KA-1 and C_KA-2 below.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(b)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Transport requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-1: A wallet unit shall use a wallet instance attestation during the issuance of person identification data, qualified or non-qualified electronic attestations of attributes, or electronic attestations of attributes, provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-2: A wallet provider shall verify the integrity of the wallet instance and sign or seal the wallet instance attestation.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-2.1: Where a wallet provider issues a wallet instance attestation, the difference between the time when the wallet provider verified the integrity of the wallet instance and the time they will indicate in the 'exp' header parameter of the issued wallet instance attestation shall be less than 24 hours.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-2.2: The wallet provider shall ensure that a wallet unit contains wallet instance attestations as needed for issuance of person identification data and electronic attestations of attributes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-3: During issuance, a wallet unit shall send a wallet instance attestation to the authorisation server in the pushed authorisation request and the token request, as specified in OID4VCI.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-3.1: A wallet unit shall send the wallet instance attestation together with a Proof-of-Possession ('PoP') as specified in Appendix E of OID4VCI.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-3.2: A wallet unit shall send the same wallet instance attestation to only one authorisation server.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-3.2.1: Where a wallet provider uses the 'per-issuer reuse' option specified in R_WIA_1 below, a wallet unit may send a wallet instance attestation to the same authorisation server multiple times.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-3.2.2: Where a wallet provider does not use the 'per-issuer reuse' option, a wallet unit shall use a wallet instance attestation in at most one issuance process.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-4: Where an authorisation server receives a wallet instance attestation, it shall verify the signature of the wallet instance attestation using the public key in the signing certificate included in the 'x5c' parameter in the JOSE header of the wallet instance attestation.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-4.1: The authorisation server shall also verify that this signing certificate can be verified with a trust anchor on the list of wallet providers referred to in Article 5 of Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980, potentially using intermediate certificates included in the x5c parameter.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-4.2: The authorisation server shall verify that the wallet instance attestation has not expired.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR-WIA-4.3: The authorisation server shall verify the signature of the PoP under the public key present in the 'cnf' claim.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-1: A wallet unit shall use a key attestation during the issuance of person identification data and during the issuance of device-bound qualified or non-qualified electronic attestations of attributes, or electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-1.1: A wallet unit shall not use a key attestation during the issuance of non-device-bound qualified or non-qualified electronic attestations of attributes, or electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-2: A wallet provider shall provide a wallet unit with different key attestations for the WSCD of the wallet unit and for each of its keystores.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-2.1: The wallet provider shall sign or seal a key attestation, after the wallet provider has verified that the keys attested to in the key attestation are stored in the WSCD of the wallet unit or keystore described in the key attestation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-2.2: A key attestation shall contain at least one attested public key.
