The number of keys in the key attestation sent to a credential issuer should not exceed the maximum batch size specified by that credential issuer in its Credential Issuer metadata; see ETSI TS 119 472-3 <a href="#ntr3-L_202601731EN.001802-E0003" id="ntc3-L_202601731EN.001802-E0003">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">3</span>)</a>, parameter 'credential_configurations_supported. credential_metadata.credential_reuse_policy.options.batch_size'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-2.3: A wallet provider shall include a public key (corresponding to a private key stored in the WSCD or keystore of the wallet unit) in at most one key attestation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-2.4: A wallet unit shall use a key attestation during at most one credential issuance or re-issuance process.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-2.5: A wallet provider shall ensure that a wallet unit is in possession of key attestations as needed for issuance of person identification data and device-bound electronic attestations of attributes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-3: Where needed during issuance, a wallet unit shall include a key attestation in the field 'proofs' of a Credential Request to the credential issuer, as specified in OID4VCI, in a proof of either type 'jwt' or type 'attestation'.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA_3.1: Where a wallet unit includes a key attestation in a 'jwt' element, it shall sign or seal the key attestation using the private key corresponding to the public key at index 0 of the 'attested_keys' array within the 'key_attestation' object.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-4: Where a credential issuer issues device-bound credentials, it shall indicate in the 'proof_types_supported' parameter in its Issuer Credential Metadata, as specified in Section 12.2.4 OID4VCI, that it supports both the 'jwt' and the 'attestation' proof type for key attestations that shall include the 'key_attestations_required' object.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-4.1: Where a credential issuer issues non-device-bound credentials, it shall omit the 'proof_types_supported' and the 'cryptographic_binding_methods_supported' parameters in the Credential Issuer Metadata.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-5: Where a credential issuer receives a key attestation in a 'jwt' or an 'attestation' proof type, it shall verify the signature of the key attestation under the public key in the signing certificate included in the 'x5c' parameter in the JOSE header of the key attestation and that this signing certificate with a trust anchor on the list for wallet providers referred to in Article 5 of Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980, potentially using intermediate certificates included in the 'x5c' parameter.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-6: Where a credential issuer receives a key attestation in a 'jwt' proof type, it shall verify the signature of the 'jwt' element under the key at index 0 of the 'attested_keys' array within the 'key_attestation' object included in the 'jwt' element.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-6.1: The credential issuer shall verify that the 'nonce' field of the 'jwt' element includes a valid c_nonce from their nonce_endpoint, as specified in OID4VCI.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-7: Where a credential issuer receives a key attestation in an 'attestation' proof type, it shall verify that the 'key_attestation' object includes a valid c_nonce from their nonce_endpoint.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>TR_KA-8: A provider of person identification data shall ensure that person identification data is bound to a public key that originates from a key attestation mentioning a WSCD.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(c)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Content requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>C_WIA-1: A wallet instance attestation shall include the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the 'wallet_name' claim specified in Appendix E of OID4VCI, where its value shall be the identifier of the wallet solution that can be found on the list of wallet providers referred to in Article 5 of Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a 'wallet_version' claim <a href="#ntr4-L_202601731EN.001802-E0004" id="ntc4-L_202601731EN.001802-E0004">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">4</span>)</a>, that shall be a string whose value shall be the version of the wallet solution.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a 'wallet_solution_certification_information' claim, that shall be a JSON object containing information about the conformity assessment body that certified the wallet solution, the certification number, as applicable, and other relevant details as regards certification.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a 'client_status' claim, containing two sub-fields:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'status': a status list reference as specified in Appendix E of OID4VCI that represents the revocation status of the wallet instance. See section (e) below for details;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'exp': a NumericDate, as specified in RFC 7519, defining the time until which the wallet provider will maintain the revocation status at the status list index referenced in 'status'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the 'exp' claim specified in Appendix E of OID4VCI.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: The 'client_status.status' claim in a wallet instance attestation represents the revocation status of the wallet instance, not the revocation status of the attestation itself. As described in R_WIA-1 below, a wallet provider can decide to scope each wallet instance attestation to a specific authorisation server based on the fact that all attestations sent to that server contain the same index value in the 'client_status.status' entry.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: The 'idx' value in the 'status' claim can be used as a (pairwise) unique identifier of the wallet instance and of the wallet unit.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>C_WIA-2: A wallet instance attestation should also include the 'wallet_link' claim specified in Appendix E of OID4VCI and the value of this claim shall be a URI where further information about the wallet solution can be obtained.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>C_WIA-3: An authorization server shall not interpret the 'exp' parameter in the top level of a wallet instance attestation as the end of the revocation maintenance period of the wallet instance.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: The 'exp' parameter in the top level of a wallet instance attestation denotes when the attestation itself expires.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>C_KA-1: A key attestation shall include:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the 'key_storage' and 'user_authentication' claims specified in Appendix D of OID4VCI.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The 'key_storage' and 'user_authentication' attributes shall have value 'iso_18045_high' where a key attestation mentions a WSCD,</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the 'certification' claim specified in Appendix D of OID4VCI, containing a URL where information can be obtained about the certification achieved by the WSCD or keystore, indicatively the scheme such as Common Criteria or GlobalPlatform, the evaluated requirements such as the applicable Protection Profile, and the evaluation level.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>It shall be possible to determine from this information whether the key storage is a WSCD.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>a 'key_storage_status' claim, containing two sub-fields:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'status': a status list reference as specified in Appendix D.1 of OID4VCI. The value represents either the revocation status of the WSCD or keystore type used to store the attested keys, or -- under the per-key-attestation index option -- the revocation status of an individual wallet unit's WSCD or keystore. See R_KA_1 below for the available index assignment options;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'exp': a NumericDate, as specified in RFC 7519, defining the time until which the wallet provider will maintain the revocation status at the status list index referenced in 'status'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the 'exp' claim specified in Appendix D of OID4VCI.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span> on the 'idx' value in the 'key_storage_status.status' claim in a key attestation: Where the wallet provider uses the 'type-shared index' option (see R_KA_1 below), all key attestations for the same type of WSCD or keystore share the same status list index. Therefore, the 'idx' value is not unique per wallet unit. On the contrary, where the wallet provider uses the 'per-key-attestation index' option, the 'idx' value is unique to the wallet unit (or pairwise unique per credential issuer). In all cases, however, a credential issuer shall not use the 'idx' value in a key attestation as a wallet unit identifier, but shall instead use the 'idx' value in a wallet instance attestation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>C_KA-2: Where a key attestation is sent in an 'attestation' proof type, it shall also include a valid c_nonce as specified in Appendix F.3 of OID4VCI.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>C_KA-3: A credential issuer shall not interpret the 'exp' parameter in the top level of a key attestation as the end of the revocation maintenance period of the WSCD or keystore.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: The 'exp' parameter in the top level of a key attestation denotes when the key attestation itself expires.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(d)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Life cycle requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>This Annex specifies the following Credential Issuer metadata parameters:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'preferred_client_status_period': OPTIONAL. An integer specifying the preferred remaining status maintenance period of the wallet instance attestation to be presented by the wallet unit during issuance, in seconds. The remaining status maintenance period is defined as the value of 'client_status.exp' in the attestation minus the time of reception of the attestation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'preferred_key_storage_status_period': OPTIONAL. An integer specifying the preferred remaining status maintenance period of the key attestation to be presented by the wallet unit during issuance, in seconds. The remaining status maintenance period is defined as the value 'key_storage_status.exp' in the attestation minus the time of reception of the attestation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_WIA-1: An authorisation server may communicate its preferences for the remaining status maintenance period in wallet instance attestations by including the 'preferred_client_status_period' metadata parameter in their Credential Issuer metadata endpoint as specified in Section 12.2.2 of OID4VCI.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_WIA-1.1: This field shall be placed at the top level of the Credential Issuer metadata.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_WIA_2: An authorisation server shall not interpret the 'exp' parameter in the top level of a wallet instance attestation as the end of the revocation maintenance period of the wallet instance.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_WIA_3: Where a wallet provider signs or seals a wallet instance attestation, it shall maintain the revocation status of the relevant wallet instance until the 'wallet_instance_status.exp' indicated in that wallet instance attestation has passed.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_KA-1: Where a key attestation is needed, a credential issuer may communicate its preferences for the remaining status maintenance period in key attestations by including the 'preferred_key_storage_status_period' metadata parameter specified above in their Credential Issuer Metadata endpoint as specified in Section 12.2.2 of OID4VCI.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_KA-1.1: This field shall be placed within the 'key_attestations_required' object as specified in Section 12.2.4 of OID4VCI.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_KA-2: A wallet provider shall choose the technical validity period of the key attestations it issues.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_KA-3: Where a wallet provider signs or seals a key attestation, it shall maintain the revocation status of the relevant WSCD or keystore until the 'key_storage_status.exp' indicated in that key attestation has passed.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_GEN-1: A wallet provider shall ensure that a wallet unit can always present wallet unit attestations and key attestations whose 'client_status.exp' and 'key_storage_status.exp' (respectively) are at least 31 days in the future at the time of presentation to an authorization server or credential issuer.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE:</span> This guarantees that providers of person identification data can rely on revocation chaining without being forced to issue short-lived person identification data.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_GEN-2: A wallet provider shall ensure that a wallet unit fetches the Credential Issuer metadata during issuance.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_GEN_2.1: If a 'preferred_key_storage_status_period' field is included in this metadata, then the wallet unit shall send a key attestation with ('key_storage_status.exp' – current time) – 'preferred_key_storage_status_period' as small as possible but non-negative. If no such key attestation is available to the wallet unit, it shall obtain a new key attestation from the wallet provider that satisfies 'key_storage_status.exp' – current time ≥ 'preferred_key_storage_status_period'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_GEN_2.2: If a 'preferred_client_status_period' field is included in this metadata, then the wallet unit shall send a wallet instance attestation with ('client_status.exp' – current time) – 'preferred_client_status_period' as small as possible but non-negative. If no such wallet instance attestation is available to the wallet unit, it shall request a new wallet instance attestation from the wallet provider that satisfies 'client_status.exp' – current time ≥ 'preferred_client_status_period'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_GEN-3: The technical validity period of person identification data shall end before both the 'client_status.exp' of the wallet instance attestation and the 'key_storage_status.exp' of the key attestation sent to the provider of person identification data in the issuance process.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>LC_GEN-4: A provider of person identification data having a technical validity period of more than 24 hours shall check the revocation status of both the wallet instance attestation and the key attestation received during issuance at least once every 24 hours, during the technical validity period of the person identification data. Where either is revoked, the provider shall revoke the person identification data.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(e)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Revocation requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_GEN-1: A wallet provider shall use Token Status Lists (specified in IETF Token Status List) as the revocation mechanism for both key attestations and wallet instance attestations, as specified in OID4VCI Appendix D and E, respectively.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: To improve scalability of its status lists, a wallet provider can use the following optimisations:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Dividing the status list into multiple chunks, where the wallet providers have a sizeable number of users and issued attestations. Many chunking strategies exist, for example based on a fixed size or by time period. The strategy for chunking is left to wallet provider discretion. Considerations can include the size of a status list for downloading and the privacy of the user.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Having multiple status lists.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Compressing a status list to reduce its size.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-1: A wallet provider may assign the same value to the 'idx' claim in the 'client_status.status' claim in all wallet instance attestations that a given wallet unit presents to the same authorization server. This is called the 'per-issuer reuse' option. If this option is used:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-1.1: The wallet unit shall maintain state on which index value it has used for each authorization server it has previously interacted with, and shall request a wallet instance attestation containing that same index value when interacting with the same authorization server again.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-1.2: Where a wallet provider receives a request for a wallet instance attestation containing a specific index value, it shall verify that the requesting wallet unit has received that index value previously, before issuing a new wallet instance attestation with that index value.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-1.3: The wallet unit shall not reuse the same index value for interactions with different authorization servers.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: If the 'per-issuer reuse' option is used, the wallet provider will be able to determine how many authorization servers a wallet unit has interacted with and how frequently it interacts with each.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-2: In its privacy policy, a wallet provider shall document whether it uses the 'per-issuer reuse' option for wallet instance revocation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-3: Where a wallet provider does not use the 'per-issuer reuse' option, the wallet provider shall assign a fresh, unlinkable index value to each wallet instance attestation it issues.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-4: Where a wallet unit must be revoked, a wallet provider shall revoke the index values in the 'client_status.status' claim in all wallet instance attestations associated with that wallet unit.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_WIA-5: A wallet provider shall take into consideration the scale of its deployment and the underlying architecture when determining the size of its wallet instance attestation status lists, ensuring that they are sufficiently large to prevent correlation and protect user privacy. At a minimum, a status list shall, where possible, relate to at least 10 000 attestations.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_KA_1: A wallet provider shall choose one of the following index assignment options for the 'key_storage_status.status' claim in a key attestation:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Option 1, called 'type-shared index', in which all key attestations attesting keys stored in the same type of WSCD or keystore contain the same index value in 'key_storage_status.status'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Option 2, called 'per-key-attestion index', in which a key attestation attesting keys stored in an individual WSCD or keystore contains a (pairwise) unique index value in 'key_storage_status.status'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: Where a wallet provider uses option 1, a single revocation action invalidates all key attestations of the affected type across all wallet units. Moreover, because all key attestations for the same type of WSCD or keystore share a single status list index, the number of entries in a key attestation status list reflects the number of WSCD or keystore types supported by the wallet provider, not the number of deployed wallet units. The privacy considerations that motivate the minimum status list size for wallet instance status lists therefore do not apply to key attestation status lists under option 1, provided that there are sufficiently many wallet units using the same type of WSCD or keystore.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: Where a wallet provider uses option 2, each index represents the revocation state of the specific WSCD or keystore attested in that key attestation.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: Where a wallet provider uses option 2, a specific WSCD or keystore can also be revoked on User request.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_KA-2: Where a wallet provider uses option 2, the wallet provider may optionally use the 'per-issuer reuse' option described in R_WIA-1. If the wallet provider uses this option, the requirements R_WIA-1 - R_WIA-3 shall apply mutatis mutandis.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_KA-3: Where a wallet provider uses option 2, the wallet provider shall take into consideration the scale of its deployment and the underlying architecture when determining the size of its key attestation status lists, ensuring that they are sufficiently large to prevent correlation and protect user privacy. At a minimum, a status list shall, where possible, relate to at least 10 000 key attestations.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>R_KA-4: Where a wallet provider uses option 1 (type-shared index), the wallet provider shall only revoke a 'key_storage_status.status' entry if the type of WSCD or keystore has a security vulnerability.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(f)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements regarding signature algorithms</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>SA-1: For signing wallet instance attestations, key attestations, related proof-of-possessions and token status lists, one of the following algorithms shall be used:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ES256 (ECDSA with SHA-256 and P-256)</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ES384 (ECDSA with SHA-384 and P-384)</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ES512 (ECDSA with SHA-512 and P-521)</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>SA-2: A wallet provider shall choose which of the algorithms mentioned in SA-1 it will use.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>SA-3: An authorization server or a credential issuer (as specified in OID4VCI) shall support all of the algorithms mentioned in SA-1.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

</div>

</div>

’

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<a href="#ntc1-L_202601731EN.001802-E0001" id="ntr1-L_202601731EN.001802-E0001">(<span class="oj-super">1</span>)</a>  RFC 7519: JSON Web Token (JWT), May 2015.

<a href="#ntc2-L_202601731EN.001802-E0002" id="ntr2-L_202601731EN.001802-E0002">(<span class="oj-super">2</span>)</a>  OpenID for Verifiable Credential Issuance v1.0, <https://openid.net/specs/openid-4-verifiable-credential-issuance-1_0.html>.

<a href="#ntc3-L_202601731EN.001802-E0003" id="ntr3-L_202601731EN.001802-E0003">(<span class="oj-super">3</span>)</a>  ETSI, ‘Electronic Signatures and Infrastructures (ESI); JAdES digital signatures; Part 3: JAdES levels and baseline profiles’, ETSI TS 119 472-3, V1.1.1, March 2026.

<a href="#ntc4-L_202601731EN.001802-E0004" id="ntr4-L_202601731EN.001802-E0004">(<span class="oj-super">4</span>)</a>  This claim is defined in this CIR as it is not part of the OID4VCI specification.

</div>

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<div id="L_202601731EN.002601.fmx">

<div id="anx_IV" class="eli-container">

ANNEX IV

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

ANNEX II

List of standards referred to in Article 8

The technical specifications set out in clauses 2 to 6 of ETSI TS 119 472-1 V1.2.1 (2026-02) apply. They shall be read with the following adaptations:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(1)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>2.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Normative references</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>[16] ETSI EN 319 412-1 V1.6.1 (2025-06): 'Electronic Signatures and Infrastructures (ESI); Certificate Profiles; Part 1: Overview and common data structures'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>[17] ETSI TS 119 412-6 V1.1.1 (2025-09): 'Electronic Signatures and Trust Infrastructures (ESI); Certificate Profiles; Part 6: Certificate profile requirements for PID, Wallet, EAA, QEAA, and PSBEAA providers'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>[25] IETF Token Status List (TSL), draft-ietf-oauth-status-list-20: 'Token Status List', 20 April 2026.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(2)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.2.11.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>General requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-4.2.11.1-06: When a status element is used for person identification data, qualified electronic attestations of attributes, or electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source, it shall only indicate whether the attestation is revoked or not revoked and shall not support any other status values, such as suspension.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-4.2.11.1-06.1: Where an attestation is revoked, it shall be permanently revoked.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(3)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.2.13</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA short-lived</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-4.2.13-03: Where short-lived electronic attestations of attributes with a validity period of 24 hours or less are issued, revocation shall not be required.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(4)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.6.3</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements for EU EAA issued by or on behalf of a public body responsible for an authentic source (PuB-EAA)</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>PuB-EAA-4.6.2-03: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Pub-EAA-4.6.2-04: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>PuB-EAA-4.6.3-03: The PuB-EAA digital signature should contain the qualified certificate supporting the PuB-EAA digital signature.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>PuB-EAA-4.6.3-04: The qualified certificate supporting the PuB-EAA digital signature shall meet the requirements of clause 8 of ETSI TS 119 412-6 v1.1.1 and shall contain the QcType qcStatement as defined in ETSI EN 319 412-5 v2.5.1, with the value id-etsi-qct-eidaspsbeaa defined as follows:</p>
<p>id-etsi-qct-eidaspsbeaa OBJECT IDENTIFIER ::= { id-etsi-eidas2-qct-extensions 3 } -- Certificate referred to in Art.45f(1)(b) supporting the qualified electronic signature or qualified electronic seal of the public sector body referred to in Article 3, point (46) of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(5)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.2.10.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>General requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-04: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-05: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-06: The status member may contain the status_list member as specified in clause 6.2 of IETF draft-ietf-oauth-status-list-20 [25].</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-07: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-08: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-09: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-10: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-11: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-5.2.10.1-12: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(6)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.2.10.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>General requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-01: When an electronic attestation of attributes compliant with ISO/IEC mdoc uses the attestation status list mechanism as set out in EAA-6.2.10.1-02.2 or the attestation revocation list mechanism as set out in EAA-6.2.10.1-02.3, its Mobile Security Object (MSO) shall contain the status structure, as specified in EAA-6.2.10.1-17, which contains MSO revocation information.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-01.1: When implementing the identifier list mechanism, the status element shall contain the identifier_list element as set out in EAA-6.2.10.1-11.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-01.2: When implementing the status list mechanism, the status element shall contain the status_list element as set out in EAA-6.2.10.1-13.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The status structure contains a reference to an MSO revocation list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The MSO revocation list is a COSE_Sign1 structure that indicates whether a particular MSO is revoked or not.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The status structure contains all the information necessary for the wallet-relying party to determine whether the MSO revocation list is authentic.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02: The provider of person identification data, the provider of qualified electronic attestations of attributes or the provider of electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source shall use one of the following methods for revocation of person identification data, qualified electronic attestations of attributes or electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source:</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.1: Where they issue short-lived electronic attestations of attributes having a validity period of equal to or less than 24 hours, then revocation shall not be required.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.2: Use an attestation status list mechanism to encode the revocation information as a status list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.2.1: The status list mechanism revokes an MSO based on whether the bit of the issuer-defined bit position in the MSO is set to true in the status list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.2.2: The status list mechanism is specified in the token status list (draft-ietf-oauth-status-list-20) specification.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.3: Use an attestation revocation list mechanism to encode the revocation information as an identifier list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.3.1: The identifier list mechanism revokes an MSO based on whether the issuer-defined identifier in the MSO is present on the identifier list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-02.3.2: EAA-6.2.10.1-06, EAA-6.2.10.1-08, EAA-6.2.10.1-09, EAA-6.2.10.1-10 and EAA-6.2.10.1-11 specify the identifier list mechanism, based on the requirements from the token status list specification including the commonality between the status list and identifier list mechanism.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-03: When a status element is used for person identification data, qualified electronic attestations of attributes or electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source, the status 'revoked' shall be exclusively used.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-03.1: For a status list this implies that only values 'valid' and 'invalid', as specified in the token status list specification shall be used.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-03.2: For the identifier list, only revoked MSOs, as opposed to temporarily suspended MSOs, shall be put in the identifier list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-04: Where an MSO is revoked, the MSO shall be permanently revoked.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-05: Verification of the MSO revocation list is optional for the wallet-relying party and where applicable, verification shall comply with the verification requirements specified in the token status list specification and the status structure specification set out in in EAA-6.2.10.1-01.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-05.1: Where a wallet-relying party needs to be able to verify the revocation status of person identification data or electronic attestations of attributes, it shall support both the attestation status list mechanism and the attestation revocation list mechanism set out in EAA-6.2.10.1-02.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-06: The identifier_list and status_list in the MSO may contain the certificate element.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-06.1: Where the certificate element is present, it shall contain a certificate containing the public key that signed or sealed the top-level certificate in the x5chain element in the MSO revocation list structure.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-06.1.1: The wallet-relying party instance shall use that certificate as a trust anchor for the verification of the x5chain element in the MSO revocation list structure.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-06.2: Where the certificate element is not present, the top-level certificate in the x5chain element in the MSO revocation list structure shall be signed or sealed by the certificate used to sign the certificate in the x5chain element of the MSO.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-06.2.1: The wallet-relying party instance shall use that certificate as a trust anchor for the verification of the x5chain element in the MSO revocation list structure.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-07: An MSO revocation list shall be implemented in accordance with the token status list specification as a status list token in CWT format.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-08: For the MSO revocation list for the identifier list and status list mechanism, the following requirements apply:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the exp claim shall be present.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the ttl claim may be present.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the aggregation_uri claim in the IdentifierList or StatusList claim may be present and the provider of person identification data, the provider of qualified electronic attestations of attributes, or the provider of electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body responsible for an authentic source may use the aggregation_uri claim to indicate support for the aggregation mechanism as specified in the token status list specification.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the CWT shall be a COSE_Sign1 object using one of the following signature algorithms for calculating the signature:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(a)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'ES256' (ECDSA with curve NIST P-256 and SHA-256);</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(b)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'ES384' (ECDSA with curve NIST P-384 and SHA-384);</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(c)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'ES512' (ECDSA with curve NIST P-521 and SHA-512);</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(d)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'ESB256' (ECDSA with curve brainpoolP256r1 and SHA-256);</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(e)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'ESB384' (ECDSA with curve brainpoolP384r1 and SHA-384);</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(f)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>'ESB512' (ECDSA with curve brainpoolP512r1 and SHA-512);</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the CWT shall contain the x5chain in the protected header that contains the certificate or chain of certificates to verify the signature of the MSO revocation list.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the extended key usage of the object identifier specified in the token status list specification may be used for the status list and the identifier list signing certificate and the wallet-relying party instances may support the extended key usage of object identifiers specified in the token status list specification and for the object identifier, the provider of qualified electronic attestations of attributes, or the provider of electronic attestations of attributes provided by or on behalf of a public sector body are not to make the extended key usage field critical when using the extended key usage OID specified in the token status list specification.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-09: In deviation to the requirements of the token status list specification, for the identifier list mechanism the following requirements apply:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the value of the type claim shall be 'application/identifierlist+cwt';</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the StatusList claim shall not be present in the CWT claims set;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the IdentifierList structure defined in EAA-6.2.10.1-11 shall be present as a claim in the CWT claims set using the key 65530.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-10: The IdentifierList structure shall be a CBOR structure with the following CDDL:</p>
<p>IdentifierList = {</p>
<p>'identifiers': { * Identifier =&gt; IdentifierInfo },</p>
<p>? 'aggregation_uri': Aggregation_uri</p>
<p>* tstr =&gt; RFU</p>
<p>}</p>
<p>IdentifierInfo = { tstr/int =&gt; RFU }</p>
<p>Identifier = bstr</p>
<p>Aggregation_uri = tstr</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-10.1: Where the identifier in the IdentifierList is present the MSO that contains the identifier in the status element is revoked.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-10.2: The Aggregation_uri claim is specified in section 9.2 of the token status list specification.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-10.3: The identifier list content-type shall be 'application/identifierlist+cwt' set out in the requirements specified in section 8.2 of token status list specification.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-11: The following requirements apply to the identifier_list element in the MSO (see EAA-6.2.10.1-17).</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-11.1: The identifier_list element is a CBOR structure with the following CDDL:</p>
<p>IdentifierListInfo = {</p>
<p>'id': Identifier ,</p>
<p>'uri': URI,</p>
<p>? 'certificate': Certificate</p>
<p>* tstr =&gt; RFU</p>
<p>}</p>
<p>URI = tstr</p>
<p>Certificate = bstr</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-11.2: REV-11.2: To prevent the identifier from being used as a correlation across presentations, it shall be unique per MSO.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-12: The following requirements apply to the status list:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-12.1: The bits element in the StatusList structure shall be set to 1.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-13: The following requirements apply to the status_list element in the MSO (see EAA-6.2.10.1-17):</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-13.1: The status_list element shall follow the requirements for the StatusListInfo structure as specified in the token status list specification and the optional certificate element defined in EAA-6.2.10.1-06 shall be added.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-13.2: To prevent the status index from being a correlation across presentations, the combination of status index and URI shall be unique per MSO.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-14: The wallet provider shall use the second (EAA-6.2.10.1-02.2) or the third (EAA-6.2.10.1-02.3) of the methods specified in EAA-6.2.10.1-02 for the revocation of a wallet instance attestation (WIA) and for the revocation of a key attestation (KA).</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-15: The wallet provider shall implement the attestation revocation mechanisms specified in EAA-6.2.10.1-02 in their wallet solution.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
