<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-16: The provider of person identification data and the provider of electronic attestations of attributes shall support both the attestation status list mechanism and the attestation revocation list mechanism specified in EAA-6.2.10.1-02 for verifying the revocation status of a wallet instance attestation (WIA) and of a key attestation (KA).</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAA-6.2.10.1-17: The status structure in the MSO shall be a CBOR structure with the following CDDL:</p>
<p>Status = {</p>
<p>? 'identifier_list' : IdentifierListInfo,</p>
<p>? 'status_list' : StatusListInfo,</p>
<p>* tstr =&gt; RFU</p>
<p>}</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

</div>

’

</div>

</div>

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<div id="L_202601731EN.003201.fmx">

<div id="anx_V" class="eli-container">

ANNEX V

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

ANNEX III

Technical specifications referred to in Article 10

|     |                           |
|-----|---------------------------|
| —   | Technical specifications: |

|     |                                                     |
|-----|-----------------------------------------------------|
| —   | Clause 4.2.5 of ETSI TS 119 472-3 V1.1.1 (2026-03). |

</div>

</div>

’

</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="L_202601731EN.003301.fmx">

<div id="anx_VI" class="eli-container">

ANNEX VI

Annex IV of Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2979 is amended as follows:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>1.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>point 1, is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘1.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Mandatory signature or seal format:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(a)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>PAdES (PDF Advanced Electronic Signature) as specified in ETSI EN 319 142-1 V1.2.1 (2024-01); Electronic Signatures and Infrastructures (ESI); PAdES digital signatures; Part 1: Building blocks and PAdES baseline signatures.’;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>2.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>point 3, is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘3.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Application programming interface:</p>
<p>ETSI TS 119 432 v1.3.1 (2026-03) clauses 6.4.3, A.6, A.7 and A.8.’.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<div id="L_202601731EN.003401.fmx">

<div id="anx_VII" class="eli-container">

ANNEX VII

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

| TrustMarkResourceURL | URL of the EU Digital Identity Wallet Trust Mark graphics and user info resources in the wallet user interface. | URL | mandatory |
| ListOfCertifiedWalletsURL | URL of the public list of certified wallet solutions in EU as set out in Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2025/849<a href="#ntr1-L_202601731EN.003602-E0001" id="ntc1-L_202601731EN.003602-E0001"> (<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>. | URL | mandatory |
| ListOfCertifiedWalletsQRCode | QR Code containing the information of ListOfCertifiedWalletsURL | ISO-8859-1 Byte mode QR code | optional |
| WalletSolutionInfoPageURL | URL to the information page of the certified wallet solution in the list of certified wallet solution page from the ListOfCertifiedWalletsURL URL appended with a '?' and the WalletSolutionID identifier of the wallet solution. | URL | mandatory |
| WalletSolutionInfoPageQRCode | QR Code containing the information of WalletSolutionInfoPageURL | ISO-8859-1 Byte mode QR code | optional |
| WalletVerifierToolURL\* | URL pointing to the wallet verification tool <span class="oj-italic">/.well-known/openid-credential-issuer</span> endpoint used for retrieval of the attestation provider metadata. | URL | optional |
| <a href="#ntc1-L_202601731EN.003602-E0001" id="ntr1-L_202601731EN.003602-E0001">(<span class="oj-super oj-note-tag">1</span>)</a>  Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2025/849 of 6 May 2025 laying down rules for the application of Regulation (EU) No 910/2014 of the European Parliament and of the Council as regards the submission of information to the Commission and to the Cooperation Group for the list of certified European Digital Identity Wallets (OJ L, 2025/849, 7.5.2025, ELI: http://data.europa.eu/eli/reg_impl/2025/849/oj). |  |  |  |

</div>

</div>

’

</div>

</div>

</div>

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<div id="L_202601731EN.003701.fmx">

<div id="anx_X" class="eli-container">

ANNEX X

Annex II to Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2980 is amended as follows:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>1.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Annex II, Section 1, point 1(i), is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘(i)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>one or more certificates compliant with standard ETSI EN 319 412-2 V2.4.1 (2025-06) or standard ETSI EN 319 412-3 V1.3.1 (2023-09) which can be used to verify the signature or seal created by the registrar on the register data and for which the certified identity data include the name of the registrar, and where applicable, the registration number of the registrar, as provided in points (c) and (d), respectively;’;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>2.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Annex II, Section 2, point 1(h), is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘(h)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>one or more certificates compliant with standard ETSI EN 319 412-2 V2.4.1 (2025-06) or standard ETSI EN 319 412-3 V1.3.1 (2023-09) that can be used to authenticate and validate wallet unit attestations the wallet provider has issued, and for which the certified identity data includes the name, and where applicable, the registration number of the wallet provider, as specified in points (a) and (b), respectively;’;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>3.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Annex II, Section 3, point 1(h), is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘(h)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>one or more certificates compliant with standard ETSI EN 319 412-2 V2.4.1 (2025-06) or standard ETSI EN 319 412-3 V1.3.1 (2023-09) that can be used to verify the signature or seal created by the provider of person identification data on the person identification data it provides, and for which the certified identity data include the name, and where applicable, the registration number of the person identification data provider, as specified in points (a) and (b), respectively.’;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Annex II, Section 4, point 1(g), is replaced by the following:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘(g)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>one or more certificates compliant with standard ETSI EN 319 412-2 V2.4.1 (2025-06) or standard ETSI EN 319 412-3 V1.3.1 (2023-09) that can be used to verify the signature or seal created by the provider of wallet-relying party access certificates on the access certificate it provides to wallet-relying parties, with, where applicable, the information required to distinguish wallet-relying party access certificates from other certificates.’;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Annex II, Section 5 is added as follows:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>‘5.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><span>Notifications of information on providers of wallet-relying party registration certificates</span>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>1.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Member States shall provide the following information to the Commission on providers of wallet-relying party registration certificates:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(a)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the name of the provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(b)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>where applicable, a registration number of the provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(c)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the Member State in which the provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates is established;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(d)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the contact email and contact phone number of the provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates, for matters related to the registration certificates it provides to wallet-relying parties;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(e)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>where applicable, the URL of the webpage of the provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates that contains additional information about the provider and the registration certificates it provides to wallet-relying parties;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(f)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>the URL of the webpage that contains the policies, terms and conditions that apply to the provision and use of the registration certificates it provides to wallet-relying parties;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(g)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>one or more certificates compliant with standard ETSI EN 319 412-2 V2.4.1 (2025-06) or standard ETSI EN 319 412-3 V1.3.1 (2023-09) that can be used to verify the signature or seal created by the provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates on the registration certificates it provides to wallet-relying parties, with, where applicable, the information required to distinguish wallet-relying party registration certificates from other certificates.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>2.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><span>The information referred to in point 1 shall be provided per provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates.’</span></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</div>

</div>

<td data-valign="top"><p>3.</p></td>

<div id="L_202601731EN.004001.fmx">

<div id="anx_XII" class="eli-container">

ANNEX XII

<div>

‘

<div>

<div>

ANNEX II

Technical specifications referred to in Article 5

The technical specification in Annex C to ISO/IEC 18013-7:2025 shall apply.

The technical specifications in clauses 4.1, 4.2, 5, and 6 of ETSI TS 119 472-2 V1.2.1 (2026-03), shall apply with the following adaptations, including the insertion of a new clause 4.3:

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(1)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Scope</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The present document specifies two profiles of protocols for allowing Relying Parties (RP hereinafter) to request EAAPs or Personal Identification Data (PID hereinafter) to the EUDI Wallet, and the EUDI Wallet to send the requested EAAPs/PIDs to the RP. Each of the profiles supports two transmission mechanisms, namely: API-mediated and non-API mediated, as shown below:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(a)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>A profile is built on:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5 [10] for non-API mediated transmission mechanism only, and</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Annex C of ISO/IEC 18013-7 [16] for API-mediated transmission mechanism.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>This profile is named ISO/IEC-mdoc profile and it is defined in clause 5 of the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(b)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>A profile is built on:</p>
<p>OpenID4VC-HAIP [11] for both API-mediated and non-API mediated transmission mechanisms, as follows:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Sections 5, 5.1, 5.3, 7, and 8 of [11] for transmission via Redirects or non-API mediated transmission mechanism, and</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Sections 5, 5.2, 5.3, 7, and 8 of [11] for API-mediated transmission mechanism.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>This profile is named OpenID4VC-HAIP profile and it is defined in clause 6 of the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(2)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>2.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Normative references</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>[15] ISO 639: 'Language code'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>[16] ISO/IEC 18013-7:2025 'Personal identification – ISO – compliant driving licence – Part 7: Mobile driving licence (mDL) add-on functions'.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(3)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP implementation based on SD-JWT VC</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP-SD-JWT VC-04: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(4)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP implementation based on ISO/IEC-mdoc</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP-ISO/IEC-mdoc-01: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">Note2</span>: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP-ISO/IEC-mdoc-02: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(5)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.3</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP implementation with mediating API</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP-API-GEN-01: The EUDI Wallet shall support a mediating API that supports both protocols defined in clause 5.2 of [11] and in Annex C of [16].</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: Where the device on which the EUDI Wallet is installed does not support both protocols, this requirement cannot be met and triggers a non-conformity due to underlying operating systems and browsers not implementing the necessary interoperability features.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>EAAP-API-GEN-02: The EUDI Wallet shall support a mediating API at least for PIDs and EAA types registered in the catalogue of schemes as defined in Implementing Regulation (EU) 2025/1569, and at least for all the formats defined in Annex II to Implementing Regulation (EU) 2024/2979.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 1</span>: Where the underlying operating system, browser, mediating APIs, or any other technical layer outside the control of the EUDI Wallet restricts, filters, preselects, or otherwise constrains the credential formats, and the PID and EAA types registered in the catalogue of schemes, this requirement cannot be met. Where such a restriction prevents the EUDI Wallet from supporting a registered credential type or format, the resulting non-conformity is attributable to the failure of those underlying operating systems, browsers, mediating API, or other relevant technical layer to provide the necessary interoperability features.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(6)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>4.4</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Wallet-relying party validation and overasking checks</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>WRP-VALIDATION-01: The EUDI Wallet shall validate the wallet-relying party registration certificate received in the request before presenting any requested PID or electronic attestation of attributes to the wallet user for approval.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>WRP-VALIDATION-02: Where the validation of the wallet-relying party registration certificate fails, including where the certificate is expired, revoked, not issued by a valid trusted provider of wallet-relying party registration certificates, malformed, or cannot be cryptographically verified, the EUDI Wallet shall warn the wallet user that the wallet-relying party could not be validated and shall not present the request as successfully validated. The wallet user shall explicitly approve the request of the relying party. Silence or pre-ticked boxes shall not suffice for explicit approval.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>WRP-VALIDATION-03: The wallet provider shall determine, based on its risk analysis and security policy, whether and under which conditions specific failed validation checks may be bypassed by the wallet user.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>WRP-OVERASKING-01: The EUDI Wallet shall compare the attestations and attributes requested by the wallet-relying party with the registered attestations and attributes in the wallet-relying party registration certificate.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>WRP-OVERASKING-02: Where the wallet-relying party requests PID or electronic attestations of attributes or claims that are not covered by the wallet-relying party registration certificate, the EUDI Wallet shall clearly warn the wallet user before any disclosure. The warning shall identify that the wallet-relying party is requesting more information than it has registered. The wallet user shall explicitly approve the request of the relying party. Silence or pre-ticked boxes shall not suffice for explicit approval.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>WRP-OVERASKING-03: The wallet provider shall determine, based on its risk analysis, security policy and applicable law, whether the wallet user may continue despite such warning, whether only the subset of requested data covered by the registration certificate may be disclosed, or whether the request shall be rejected.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(7)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Introduction</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Clause 5 and its subclauses define a profile for a protocol allowing a RP to request EAAPs or PIDs to the EUDI Wallet, and the EUDI Wallet to send the requested EAAPs/PIDs to the RP using either a non-API mediated transmission mechanism, built on ISO/IEC 18013-5 [10] or an API-mediated transmission mechanism built on Annex C of ISO/IEC 18013-7 [16] for transferring the data structures defined in ISO/IEC 18013-5 [10] suitably encapsulated.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The rest of clause 5 is organised as follows:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Clause 5.2 defines requirements on support of the profile and transmission mechanisms by RPs and EUDI Wallet;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Clause 5.3 specifies requirements that are specific for the non-API mediated transmission mechanism;</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Clause 5.4 and its subclauses specify requirements that are specific for the API-mediated transmission mechanism.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(8)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements on EUDI Wallet and RP support</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-SUPPORT-01: Wallet Units, PID Providers, Attestation Providers, Wallet Providers, and Relying Parties shall not support server retrieval as specified in ISO/IEC 18013-5 [10] for requesting and presenting PID or attestation attributes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-SUPPORT-02: The EUDI Wallet shall meet the requirements defined in clauses 5.3 and 5.4 of the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-SUPPORT-04: A Relying Party should implement the profile defined in clause 5.4 of the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(9)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.3.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC-mdoc EAAP Request contents</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-04: Device requests sent to EUDI Wallets shall contain the 'requestInfo' key-value pair specified in clause 8.3.2.1.2.1 of [10]. The value of this pair shall be of type RequestInfo.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-05: The RequestInfo type shall be as the CDDL definition shows below.</p>
<p>RequestInfo = {</p>
<p>'euWrprc': bstr ; contains a registration certificate (see requirements below)</p>
<p>}</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-06: The mentioned requestInfo member shall contain a member with label 'euWrprc'</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-07: The value of the 'euWrprc' shall be a CBOR-encoded registration certificate.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-08: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 3</span>: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-09: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-10: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-REQ-11: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(10)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.3.3</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC-mdoc EAAP Response profile</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The present clause defines requirements for the DeviceResponse message type, common to the two types of transmission mechanisms (API-mediated and non-API mediated).</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 1</span>: Where a non-API mediated mechanism built on ISO/IEC 18013-5 [10] is used, an EAAP Response is exactly an instance of DeviceResponse as profiled in the present clause, Where an API-mediated mechanism built on Annex C of ISO/IEC 18013-7 [16] is used, the instance of DeviceRequest is encapsulated as specified in Annex C of [16].</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-RESP-02: The provider of person identification data and electronic attestations of attributes shall not include any data elements in the KeyAuthorizations map in the Mobile Security Object of the person identification data and electronic attestations of attributes they issue, except for data elements that are provided by the wallet-relying party in the transactional data in the mdoc request to be signed or sealed by the wallet unit using the private key of the person identification data or electronic attestations of attributes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 2</span>: As a result, wallet units cannot present any device-signed data elements to wallet-relying parties, except to signing data that the wallet-relying party provided, for example in use cases for secure user authentication.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 3</span>: ISO/IEC 18013-5:2021 does not specify how wallet-relying parties can include transactional data in an mdoc request. The inclusion of transactional data in an mdoc request shall take place with the addition of technical specifications.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-5-RESP-03: Providers of person identification data shall not allow the private key of the person identification data to sign data elements that are provided by the wallet-relying party in the transactional data in the mdoc request.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(11)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.4</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements for API mediated mechanism</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.4.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-7-related requirements</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>The present clause defines requirements for API-mediated transmission mechanism related to the requirements defined in Annex C of [16].</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-7-API-01: The profile supporting API-mediated presentations shall comply with the requirements in Annex C of ISO/IEC 18013-7 [16] as further profiled in clauses 5.3 and 5.4 of the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-7-API-02: All the mandatory requirements defined in Annex C of [16] shall apply as further profiled in clauses 5.3 and 5.4 of the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-7-API-03: All the optional requirements defined in Annex C of [16] shall remain optional unless stated otherwise in the present document.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(12)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>5.4.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Additional requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>The present clause specifies additional requirements for API-mediated transmission mechanism.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-ADD-API-01: The EUDI Wallet shall by default disclose the presence of all stored electronic attestations of attributes’ type to the mediating API that works in accordance with Annex C of [16], but it shall not disclose the attributes and their values in these electronic attestations of attributes.</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 1</span>: The attribute value restriction applies even if such disclosure would enhance the services provided by the Operating System to the EUDI Wallet, for example, attestation selection in the context of the mediating API.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>There are the considerations related to operating systems and browsers which fall out of the control of implementers, which can be taken into account as indicated in the following notes 2 to 4:</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 2</span>: A presentation request from a Relying Party supporting Annex C of [16] can be processed by the browser and/or the Operating System for searching available EAAs, for preventing fraud targeting the User, or for troubleshooting purposes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 3</span>: A presentation request from a Relying Party supporting Annex C of [16] is expected to be processed by the browser and/or the Operating System for User security purposes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 4</span>: A presentation request from a Relying Party supporting Annex C of [16] is expected not to be processed by the browser and/or the Operating System for market analysis purposes (including as a secondary purpose) or for the browser's and/or the Operating System's internal purposes.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-ADD-API-02: Where an EUDI Wallet deletes on the User's request a PID or EAA previously disclosed to the mediating API that works in accordance with Annex C of [16], the EUDI Wallet shall disclose the fact that it no longer stores this PID or EAA to the mediating API.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-ADD-API-03: If the User uninstalls their EUDI Wallet, the EUDI Wallet shall disclose the fact that it no longer stores any previously disclosed PID(s) or EAA(s) to the mediating API that works in accordance with Annex C of [16].</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-ADD-API-04: The EUDI Wallet shall provide a global user setting to disable the disclosure of stored EAAs via a mediating API that works as is specified in ISO/IEC 18013-ADD-API-01. When this setting is disabled, the EUDI Wallet shall not advertise or respond to the API-mediated presentation or issuance requests.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>ISO/IEC 18013-ADD-API-05: EUDI Wallets shall verify in cross-device flows, using the mediating API, that the interacting device is in close physical proximity to the EUDI Wallet using a secure, direct, and user-mediated local communication channel, such as a short-range wireless communication technology to perform the physical proximity check.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE</span>: CTAP 2.3 enables to use BLE to perform the close physical proximity check and when implemented by both devices, also enables to transport the data between the devices via short-range transmission technologies. Performing both the physical proximity check and data transfer between the two devices using a local communication channel as enabled by CTAP 2.3, should be preferred by underlying operating systems, browsers, mediating APIs, or any other technical layer outside the control of the EUDI Wallet over the use of CTAP Hybrid tunnel services.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(13)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements on EUDI Wallet and RP support</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-SUPPORT-02: The EUDI Wallet shall meet the requirements specified in clause 6.5 of the present Annex.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-SUPPORT-03: The EUDI Wallet should not support the redirects-based mechanism specified in clause 6.4 for cross-device presentation flows.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 3</span>: Using this mechanism is vulnerable to attacks, such as session fixation. Mitigating such attacks is up to relying parties. Implementing this mechanism should not trigger non-compliance.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-SUPPORT-05: A Relying Party shall meet the requirements specified in clauses 6.5 of the present Annex.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 5</span>: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(14)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.3.1.</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>General requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-GEN-01: All the mandatory requirements specified in clauses 5, 5.3, 7, and 8 of HAIP [11] shall apply.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 1</span>: 'HAIP [11] Section 5' refers only to the requirements directly under the Section 5 heading. This does not include sections 5.1, 5.2, and 5.3.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-GEN-03: If the present Annex modifies a requirement in OpenID4VC-HAIP [11], the modified requirement specified by the present Annex shall prevail.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 2</span>: This would allow, for instance, to convert in mandatory an optional requirement from OpenID4VC-HAIP [11] or to extend mandatory requirements.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-GEN-04: When the format of the requested attestation complies with [10] Relying Parties and EUDI Wallets shall comply with the 'ISO mdocs' profile in [11] Section 6.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 3</span>: For clarity: the 'ISO mdocs profile' in HAIP implies that Relying Parties and EUDI Wallets must comply with the applicable requirements in [7] Annex B.2.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-GEN-05: When the format of the requested attestation complies with [2], Relying Parties and EUDI Wallets shall comply with the 'IETF SD-JWT VCs' profile in [11] Section 6.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 4</span>: For clarity: the 'IETF SD-JWT VCs profile' implies that Relying Parties and EUDI Wallets must comply with the requirements in [7] Annex B.3, as well as with the requirements in [11] Section 6.1.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(15)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.3.2.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>General requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-01: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(16)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.3.2.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Requirements for the Request Object</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-02: void</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-03: void</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-04: void</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-05: void</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-06: void.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-07: void</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>

</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-13: One of the elements of the verifier_info parameter shall include the registration certificate.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-COMMON-REQ-RO-23: The leaf certificate mentioned in OpenID4VP section 5.9.3 for use with the x509_hash Client Identifier Prefix shall be a RP access certificate as specified in ETSI TS 119 475 [14].</p></td>
</tr>

</table></td>

<td data-valign="top"><p>6.3.3</p></td>

<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(18)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">

</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.4.1</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>General requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(19)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.5.2</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Additional requirements</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-ADD-API-01: The EUDI Wallet shall by default disclose the presence of all stored EAA types to the mediating API that works in accordance with clause 5.2 of [11], but it shall not disclose the attributes and their values in these EAAs.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-ADD-API-04: If the EUDI Wallet supports a mediating API that works as is specified in OIDFVP-HAIP-ADD-API-01, it shall provide a global user setting to disable the disclosure of stored EAAs via the mediating API. When this setting is set to disabled disclosure, the EUDI Wallet should subsequently enable the User to select individual attestations to be disclosed to the mediating API.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-ADD-API-05: EUDI Wallets shall verify, in cross-device flows, using the mediating API, that the interacting device is in close physical proximity to the EUDI Wallet using a secure, direct, and user-mediated local communication channel, such as a short-range wireless communication technology to perform the physical proximity check.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p><span class="oj-italic">NOTE 5</span>: CTAP 2.3, enables to use BLE to perform the close physical proximity check and when implemented by both devices, also enables to transport the data between the devices via short-range transmission technologies. Performing both the physical proximity check and data transfer between the two devices using a local communication channel as enabled by CTAP 2.3, should be preferred by underlying operating systems, browsers, mediating APIs, or any other technical layer outside the control of the EUDI Wallet over the use of CTAP Hybrid tunnel services.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>(20)</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>6.5.3</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>Specific requirements when requesting ISO/IEC 18013-5 EAAP</p>
<table width="100%" data-border="0" data-cellspacing="0" data-cellpadding="0">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 4%" />
<col style="width: 96%" />
</colgroup>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td data-valign="top"><p>—</p></td>
<td data-valign="top"><p>OIDFVP-HAIP-ISO/IEC_18013_5_REQ-02: All requirements specified in this document applicable to Device Request and Device Response structures specified in ISO/IEC 18013-5 also apply when the EUDI Wallet receives a request for ISO/IEC mdoc presentation through an API-transmitted mechanism specified in clause C.1 of [16].</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
